Team Blog"}]}</script><script type="application/ld+json" data-next-head="">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"BlogPosting","datePublished":"10/2/2026, 7:07:38 PM","description":"Microsoft has released Security Updates (SUs) for vulnerabilities found in:\n\nExchange Server Subscription Edition (SE)\nExchange Server 2019\nExchange Server 2016\n\nSUs are available for the following specific versions of Exchange Server:\n\nExchange SE RTM\nExchange Server 2019&amp;nbsp;CU14&amp;nbsp;and&amp;nbsp;CU15 (to access, organization must be enrolled into the Period 2 ESU program)\nExchange Server 2016&amp;nbsp;CU23 (to access, organization must be enrolled into the Period 2 ESU program)\n\nThe September 2026 V2 SUs address vulnerabilities responsibly reported to Microsoft by security partners and found through Microsoft’s internal processes.\nThe difference between the original September 2026 Security Update release and this V2 release is an addition of CVE-2026-96940. Please see the download KB article for more information.\nThese vulnerabilities affect Exchange Server. Exchange Online customers are already protected from the vulnerabilities addressed by these SUs and do not need to take any action other than updating any Exchange servers or Exchange Management tools workstations in their environment.\nMore details about specific CVEs can be found in the&amp;nbsp;Security Update Guide&amp;nbsp;(filter on ‘Server Software’ under Product Family for Exchange SE and ‘ESU’ under Product Family for Exchange 2016 and 2019).\nExchange 2016 and 2019 updates are available only under the Period 2 ESU program\nExchange Server 2016 and 2019 are out of support. Only customers who enrolled in the Period 2 Extended Security Update (ESU) program are eligible to receive Exchange Server 2016 and 2019 security updates released between May and October 2026.\nIf you are not part of the Period 2 ESU program, migrate to Exchange Server Subscription Edition (SE) to keep receiving the latest security updates.\nIf you have already purchased the Period 2 ESU and need information on accessing the latest Security Updates, please contact us by sending an email to ExchangeandSfBServerESUInquiry@service.microsoft.com.\nKnown issues with this release\n\nPublished calendar (.ics) returns HTTP 500 for calendar applications | Microsoft Support – to be resolved in a future update\nContentEngine deadlock because of missing Korean WordBreaker rule files | Microsoft Support – an issue impacting customers with emails in Korean language, to be addressed in a future update\n\nIssues resolved in this release\n\nWrapper messages appear in shared mailbox inbox in hybrid environments | Microsoft Support\nAvailability (free/busy) fails for delegated mailboxes in Exchange hybrid deployments using Graph API only | Microsoft Support\n\nUpdate installation\nThe following update paths are available:\n\n\nInventory your Exchange Servers to determine which updates are needed using the Exchange Server Health Checker script. Running this script will tell you if any of your Exchange Servers are behind on updates (CUs, SUs, or manual actions).\nInstall the latest CU. Use the&amp;nbsp;Exchange Update Wizard to choose your current CU and your target CU to get directions.\nAfter setup is completed, please reboot the server and check that all Exchange services have started properly. If some services are in a disabled state, that indicates that something interrupted installation of the update. Please see the Workaround 1 in this article.\nIf you encounter errors during or after installation of Exchange Server, run the SetupAssist script. If something does not work properly after updates, see Repair failed installations of Exchange Cumulative and Security updates. Also please see File version error when you try to install Exchange Server updates.\n\nFAQs\nOur organization is in Hybrid mode with Exchange Online. Do we need to do anything?Exchange Online is already protected, but this SU needs to be installed on your Exchange servers, even if they are used only for management purposes. If you change the auth certificate after installing an SU, you should re-run the Hybrid Configuration Wizard.\nThe last SU/HU we installed is a few months old. Do we need to install all SUs in order to install the latest one?SUs are cumulative. If you are running a CU supported by the SU, you do not need to install all SUs or HUs in sequential order; simply install the latest SU. Please see&amp;nbsp;this blog post&amp;nbsp;for more information.\nDo we need to install SUs on all Exchange Servers within our organization? What about ‘Management Tools only’&amp;nbsp;machines?Our recommendation is to install SUs on&amp;nbsp;all&amp;nbsp;Exchange Servers and all servers and workstations running the Exchange Management Tools to ensure compatibility between management tools clients and servers. If you are trying to update the Exchange Management Tools in the environment with no running Exchange servers, please see&amp;nbsp;this.\nWe installed an Exchange SU or HU update and are running Windows Server 2025. How do we uninstall updates as they are not listed on Windows Server 2025?We do not recommend uninstalling Security Updates. But see Can&apos;t view or uninstall Exchange security or hotfix updates in Control Panel on Windows Server 2025 | Microsoft Support.\nOur organization does not have the Exchange 2016 and 2019 Period 2 ESU. How can we get current Exchange 2016 or 2019 updates?Since Exchange 2016 and 2019 are now out of support, only customers who have enrolled into the Period 2 ESU program (which is valid between May and October 2026) can obtain Exchange 2016 or 2019 updates released after May 2026. For all other customers still running Exchange 2016 or 2019, we recommend that you upgrade your organization to Exchange SE as soon as possible.\nThe release sequence of this specific update was a bit strange. Why is this?This specific update, which adds CVE-2026-96940, was published ahead of its intended schedule. We recommend that customers review the deployment guidance and apply the update at the earliest opportunity.\n\nWe identified the vulnerability internally and are not aware of active exploitation. ‌\nFor information about additional updates, customers should refer to our established security update channels.\n\nDocumentation may not be fully available at the time this post is published.\nThis post might receive future updates; they will be listed here (if available).\nThe Exchange Server document.getElementById('social-share-footer'); if (footerContainer) { var currentURL = window.location.href; var strippedURL = currentURL.split('?')[0]; var currentTitle = document.title; var encodedURL = encodeURIComponent(strippedURL); var encodedTitle = encodeURIComponent(currentTitle); var socialLinks = footerContainer.querySelectorAll('.social-link-sharing'); // Encode page URL and title values in aka.ms share hrefs. // https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/released-september-2026-v2-exchange-server-security-updates/4561718 / BlogMessagePage are rendered server-side but // Handlebars does not URL-encode, so a title with `\u0026`, `#`, // or spaces would produce a malformed query string. Here we // find the raw values in each href and swap them for URL- // encoded ones. Old `page.url` / `page-name` placeholders // are still replaced as a defensive fallback. socialLinks.forEach(function(link) { var currentHref = link.getAttribute('href') || ''; var newHref = currentHref; if (strippedURL) { newHref = newHref.split(strippedURL).join(encodedURL); } if (currentTitle) { newHref = newHref.split(currentTitle).join(encodedTitle); } newHref = newHref.replace('page.url', encodedURL).replace('page-name', encodedTitle); if (newHref !== currentHref) { link.setAttribute('href', newHref); } }); // Update RSS links for Blogs/Events pages. // Normalize the path segment (lower-case, drop leading slash, // take the first segment only) so `/Blogs`, `/blogs`, `/BLOGS/`, // and `/blogs/foo` all resolve to the same lower-case mapping key. var urlPath = new URL(currentURL).pathname; var rssLink = footerContainer.querySelector('.social-share-rss-footer'); if (rssLink \u0026\u0026 (/\\/blogs/i.test(urlPath) || /\\/events/i.test(urlPath))) { var pathSegment = urlPath.replace(/^\\//, '').toLowerCase().split('/')[0]; var styleMapping = { blogs: 'blog', events: 'occasion' }; var rssStyle = styleMapping[pathSegment]; if (rssStyle) { rssLink.setAttribute('href', 'https://aka.ms/MTC/RSS-Community-Footer?interaction.style=' + rssStyle + '\u0026feeds.replies=false' ); } } } \u003c/script\u003e email\u003c/span\u003e \u003c/a\u003e \u003c/div\u003e \u003cscript\u003e /* Defensive fallback. All primary interactivity (URL placeholder replacement, expand/collapse via mouse/keyboard/click/touch, aria-expanded sync, and Esc) lives in the shareRail pagescript. This inline handler exists so screen readers still get an accurate expanded/collapsed announcement and Esc still collapses the rail even in the rare case the pagescript fails to load. When the pagescript is loaded, these DOM writes are idempotent \\u2014 both scripts converge on the same aria-expanded value. */ (function () { /* aria-expanded sync via CONTAINER-level listeners only (never on the button itself). Container listeners match the CSS :hover / :focus-within triggers that already control panel visibility, so state stays in sync without interfering with the Esc handler below (button-level focus/blur was previously observed to break Esc). */ var rails = document.querySelectorAll('[data-mtc-share-rail]'); rails.forEach(function (rail) { var btn = rail.querySelector('button'); if (!btn) return; var open = function () { btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true'); }; var close = function () { var hovered = false; try { hovered = rail.matches(':hover'); } catch (err) { /* no-op */ } if (!hovered \u0026\u0026 !rail.contains(document.activeElement)) { btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); } }; rail.addEventListener('mouseenter', open); rail.addEventListener('focusin', open); rail.addEventListener('mouseleave', close); rail.addEventListener('focusout', function () { setTimeout(close, 0); }); }); document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key !== 'Escape' \u0026\u0026 e.key !== 'Esc') return; var rails = document.querySelectorAll('[data-mtc-share-rail]'); rails.forEach(function (rail) { var isOpen = false; try { isOpen = rail.matches(':hover') || rail.matches(':focus-within'); } catch (err) { /* no-op */ } if (!isOpen \u0026\u0026 rail.getAttribute('data-expanded') !== 'true') return; rail.setAttribute('data-esc-closed', 'true'); rail.removeAttribute('data-expanded'); var focused = document.activeElement; if (focused \u0026\u0026 rail.contains(focused)) { try { focused.blur(); } catch (err) { /* no-op */ } } var clearEsc = function () { rail.removeAttribute('data-esc-closed'); rail.removeEventListener('mouseleave', clearEsc); rail.removeEventListener('focusin', clearEsc); }; rail.addEventListener('mouseleave', clearEsc); rail.addEventListener('focusin', clearEsc); }); }); })(); \u003c/script\u003e gs":[]}},"canFreezeTkbArticle":{"__typename":"PolicyResult","failureReason":{"__typename":"FailureReason","message":"error.lithium.policies.tkb.action_can_freeze_article.accessDenied","key":"error.lithium.policies.tkb.action_can_freeze_article.accessDenied","args":[]}},"canReportAbuse":{"__typename":"PolicyResult","failureReason":null},"canDeleteDraft":{"__typename":"PolicyResult","failureReason":{"__typename":"FailureReason","message":"error.lithium.policies.delete_drafts.accessDenied","key":"error.lithium.policies.delete_drafts.accessDenied","args":[]}}},"contentWorkflow":{"__typename":"ContentWorkflow","state":"PUBLISH","scheduledPublishTime":null,"scheduledTimezone":null,"userContext":{"__typename":"MessageWorkflowContext","canSubmitForReview":null,"canEdit":false,"canRecall":null,"canSubmitForPublication":null,"canReturnToAuthor":null,"canPublish":null,"canReturnToReview":null,"canSchedule":false},"shortScheduledTimezone":null},"readOnly":false,"editFrozen":false,"showMoveIndicator":false,"moderationData":{"__ref":"ModerationData:moderation_data:4561718"},"teaser":"\u003cP\u003eWe have released a V2 of September 2026 Security Updates for Exchange Server SE. Exchange Server 2019 and 2016 ESU updates only.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"lia-media-object lia-media-is-center lia-media-size-default\" data-image-alt=\"\"\u003e\u003cbutton class=\"lia-media-unstyled-btn\" type=\"button\" aria-haspopup=\"true\" aria-label=\"Enlarge Image\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/t5/s/gxcuf89792/images/bS00NTYxNzE4LXpXOGk4VA?image-dimensions=370x214\u0026amp;revision=4\" width=\"370\" height=\"214\" alt=\"\" /\u003e\u003c/button\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/P\u003e","body":"\u003cP\u003eMicrosoft has released Security Updates (SUs) for vulnerabilities found in:\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server Subscription Edition (SE)\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2019\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2016\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eSUs are available for the following specific versions of Exchange Server:\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA class=\"lia-external-url\" href=\"https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/download/details.aspx?id=108855\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eExchange SE RTM\u003c/A\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2019\u0026nbsp;CU14\u0026nbsp;and\u0026nbsp;CU15 (to access, organization must be enrolled into the \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/announcing-period-2-exchange-20162019-extended-security-update-esu-program/4511603\" target=\"_blank\"\u003ePeriod 2 ESU program\u003c/A\u003e)\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2016\u0026nbsp;CU23 (to access, organization must be enrolled into the \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/announcing-period-2-exchange-20162019-extended-security-update-esu-program/4511603\" target=\"_blank\"\u003ePeriod 2 ESU program\u003c/A\u003e)\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThe September 2026 V2 SUs address vulnerabilities responsibly reported to Microsoft by security partners and found through Microsoft’s internal processes.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThe difference between the original \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/released-september-2026-exchange-server-security-updates/4554411\" target=\"_blank\"\u003eSeptember 2026 Security Update release\u003c/A\u003e and this V2 release is an addition of \u003cA href=\"https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-96940\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eCVE-2026-96940\u003c/A\u003e. Please see the download KB article for more information.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThese vulnerabilities affect Exchange Server. Exchange Online customers are already protected from the vulnerabilities addressed by these SUs and do not need to take any action other than updating any Exchange servers or Exchange Management tools workstations in their environment.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eMore details about specific CVEs can be found in the\u0026nbsp;\u003cA href=\"https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eSecurity Update Guide\u003c/A\u003e\u0026nbsp;(filter on ‘Server Software’ under Product Family for Exchange SE and ‘ESU’ under Product Family for Exchange 2016 and 2019).\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cH3 id=\"community-4561718-toc-hId--430812413\"\u003eExchange 2016 and 2019 updates are available \u003cEM\u003eonly\u003c/EM\u003e under the Period 2 ESU program\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eExchange Server 2016 and 2019 are \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/support-for-exchange-server-2016-and-exchange-server-2019-ends-today/4461192\" target=\"_blank\"\u003eout of support\u003c/A\u003e. Only customers who enrolled in the \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/announcing-period-2-exchange-20162019-extended-security-update-esu-program/4511603\" target=\"_blank\"\u003ePeriod 2 Extended Security Update (ESU) program\u003c/A\u003e are eligible to receive Exchange Server 2016 and 2019 security updates released between May and October 2026.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eIf you are not part of the Period 2 ESU program, \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/upgrading-your-organization-from-current-versions-to-exchange-server-se/4241305\" target=\"_blank\"\u003emigrate to Exchange Server Subscription Edition (SE)\u003c/A\u003e to keep receiving the latest security updates.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cEM\u003eIf you have already purchased the Period 2 ESU\u003c/EM\u003e and need information on accessing the latest Security Updates, please contact us by sending an email to \u003cA href=\"mailto:ExchangeandSfBServerESUInquiry@service.microsoft.com?subject=We%20purchased%20Exchange%20ESU%20need%20access\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener nofollow noreferrer\"\u003eExchangeandSfBServerESUInquiry@service.microsoft.com\u003c/A\u003e.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cH3 id=\"community-4561718-toc-hId-2056700420\"\u003eKnown issues with this release\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/update/2026/5126672\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003ePublished calendar (.ics) returns HTTP 500 for calendar applications | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e – to be resolved in a future update\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/update/2026/5130098\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eContentEngine deadlock because of missing Korean WordBreaker rule files | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e – an issue impacting customers with emails in Korean language, to be addressed in a future update\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cH3 id=\"community-4561718-toc-hId-249245957\"\u003eIssues resolved in this release\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/hotfix/2026/5105719\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eWrapper messages appear in shared mailbox inbox in hybrid environments | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/update/2026/5127092\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eAvailability (free/busy) fails for delegated mailboxes in Exchange hybrid deployments using Graph API only | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cH3 id=\"community-4561718-toc-hId--1558208506\"\u003eUpdate installation\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThe following update paths are available:\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"lia-media-object lia-media-is-center lia-media-size-default\" data-image-alt=\"\"\u003e\u003cbutton class=\"lia-media-unstyled-btn\" type=\"button\" aria-haspopup=\"true\" aria-label=\"Enlarge Image\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/t5/s/gxcuf89792/images/bS00NTYxNzE4LURnMG94Sg?image-dimensions=999x517\u0026amp;revision=4\" width=\"999\" height=\"517\" alt=\"\" /\u003e\u003c/button\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eInventory your Exchange Servers to determine which updates are needed using the \u003cA href=\"https://aka.ms/ExchangeHealthChecker\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eExchange Server Health Checker script\u003c/A\u003e. Running this script will tell you if any of your Exchange Servers are behind on updates (CUs, SUs, or manual actions).\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eInstall the latest CU. Use the\u0026nbsp;\u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://aka.ms/ExchangeUpdateWizard\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eExchange Update Wizard\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e to choose your current CU and your target CU to get directions.\u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eAfter setup is completed, please reboot the server and check that all Exchange services have started properly. If some services are in a disabled state, that indicates that something interrupted installation of the update. Please see the Workaround 1 in \u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/troubleshoot/exchange/client-connectivity/exchange-security-update-issues#services-dont-start-after-su-installation\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003ethis article\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e.\u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eIf you encounter errors during or after installation of Exchange Server, run the \u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://aka.ms/ExSetupAssist\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eSetupAssist script\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e. If something does not work properly after updates, see \u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://aka.ms/ExchangeFAQ\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eRepair failed installations of Exchange Cumulative and Security updates\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e. Also please see \u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/topic/file-version-error-when-you-try-to-install-exchange-server-november-2024-su-a650da30-f8fb-469d-a449-47396cab0a15\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eFile version error when you try to install Exchange Server updates\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e.\u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cH1 id=\"community-4561718-toc-hId-228239749\"\u003eFAQs\u003c/H1\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eOur organization is in Hybrid mode with Exchange Online. Do we need to do anything?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eExchange Online is already protected, but this SU needs to be installed on your Exchange servers, even if they are used only for management purposes. If you change the auth certificate after installing an SU, you should re-run the Hybrid Configuration Wizard.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eThe last SU/HU we installed is a few months old. Do we need to install all SUs in order to install the latest one?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eSUs are cumulative. If you are running a CU supported by the SU, you do not need to install all SUs or HUs in sequential order; simply install the latest SU. Please see\u0026nbsp;\u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/t5/exchange-team-blog/why-exchange-server-updates-matter/ba-p/2280770\" target=\"_blank\"\u003ethis blog post\u003c/A\u003e\u0026nbsp;for more information.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eDo we need to install SUs on all Exchange Servers within our organization? What about ‘Management Tools only’\u0026nbsp;machines?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eOur recommendation is to install SUs on\u0026nbsp;\u003cU\u003eall\u003c/U\u003e\u0026nbsp;Exchange Servers and all servers and workstations running the Exchange Management Tools to ensure compatibility between management tools clients and servers. If you are trying to update the Exchange Management Tools in the environment with no running Exchange servers, please see\u0026nbsp;\u003cA href=\"https://learn.microsoft.com/exchange/manage-hybrid-exchange-recipients-with-management-tools#update-the-exchange-server-management-tools-only-role-with-no-running-exchange-server-to-a-newer-cumulative-or-security-update\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003ethis\u003c/A\u003e.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eWe installed an Exchange SU or HU update and are running Windows Server 2025. How do we uninstall updates as they are not listed on Windows Server 2025?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eWe do not recommend uninstalling Security Updates. But see \u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/office/can-t-view-or-uninstall-exchange-security-or-hotfix-updates-in-control-panel-on-windows-server-2025\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\u003eCan't view or uninstall Exchange security or hotfix updates in Control Panel on Windows Server 2025 | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eOur organization does not have the Exchange 2016 and 2019 Period 2 ESU. How can we get current Exchange 2016 or 2019 updates?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eSince Exchange 2016 and 2019 are now \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/support-for-exchange-server-2016-and-exchange-server-2019-ends-today/4461192\" target=\"_blank\"\u003eout of support\u003c/A\u003e, only customers who have enrolled into the \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/announcing-period-2-exchange-20162019-extended-security-update-esu-program/4511603\" target=\"_blank\"\u003ePeriod 2 ESU program\u003c/A\u003e (which is valid between May and October 2026) can obtain Exchange 2016 or 2019 updates released after May 2026. For all other customers still running Exchange 2016 or 2019, we recommend that you \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/upgrading-your-organization-from-current-versions-to-exchange-server-se/4241305\" target=\"_blank\"\u003eupgrade your organization to Exchange SE\u003c/A\u003e as soon as possible.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eThe release sequence of this specific update was a bit strange. Why is this?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eThis specific update, which adds CVE-2026-96940, was published ahead of its intended schedule. We recommend that customers review the deployment guidance and apply the update at the earliest opportunity.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eWe identified the vulnerability internally and are not aware of active exploitation. ‌\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eFor information about additional updates, customers should refer to our established security update channels.\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eDocumentation may not be fully available at the time this post is published.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThis post might receive future updates; they will be listed here (if available).\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSPAN class=\"lia-text-color-12\"\u003eThe Exchange Server Team\u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003c/P\u003e","body@stringLength":"11962","rawBody":"\u003cP\u003eMicrosoft has released Security Updates (SUs) for vulnerabilities found in:\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server Subscription Edition (SE)\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2019\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2016\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eSUs are available for the following specific versions of Exchange Server:\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA class=\"lia-external-url\" href=\"https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/download/details.aspx?id=108855\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eExchange SE RTM\u003c/A\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2019\u0026nbsp;CU14\u0026nbsp;and\u0026nbsp;CU15 (to access, organization must be enrolled into the \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/announcing-period-2-exchange-20162019-extended-security-update-esu-program/4511603\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003ePeriod 2 ESU program\u003c/A\u003e)\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eExchange Server 2016\u0026nbsp;CU23 (to access, organization must be enrolled into the \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/announcing-period-2-exchange-20162019-extended-security-update-esu-program/4511603\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003ePeriod 2 ESU program\u003c/A\u003e)\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThe September 2026 V2 SUs address vulnerabilities responsibly reported to Microsoft by security partners and found through Microsoft’s internal processes.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThe difference between the original \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/released-september-2026-exchange-server-security-updates/4554411\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eSeptember 2026 Security Update release\u003c/A\u003e and this V2 release is an addition of \u003cA href=\"https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/vulnerability/CVE-2026-96940\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eCVE-2026-96940\u003c/A\u003e. Please see the download KB article for more information.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThese vulnerabilities affect Exchange Server. Exchange Online customers are already protected from the vulnerabilities addressed by these SUs and do not need to take any action other than updating any Exchange servers or Exchange Management tools workstations in their environment.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eMore details about specific CVEs can be found in the\u0026nbsp;\u003cA href=\"https://msrc.microsoft.com/update-guide/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eSecurity Update Guide\u003c/A\u003e\u0026nbsp;(filter on ‘Server Software’ under Product Family for Exchange SE and ‘ESU’ under Product Family for Exchange 2016 and 2019).\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cH3\u003eExchange 2016 and 2019 updates are available \u003cEM\u003eonly\u003c/EM\u003e under the Period 2 ESU program\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eExchange Server 2016 and 2019 are \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/support-for-exchange-server-2016-and-exchange-server-2019-ends-today/4461192\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eout of support\u003c/A\u003e. Only customers who enrolled in the \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/announcing-period-2-exchange-20162019-extended-security-update-esu-program/4511603\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003ePeriod 2 Extended Security Update (ESU) program\u003c/A\u003e are eligible to receive Exchange Server 2016 and 2019 security updates released between May and October 2026.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eIf you are not part of the Period 2 ESU program, \u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/exchange/upgrading-your-organization-from-current-versions-to-exchange-server-se/4241305\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003emigrate to Exchange Server Subscription Edition (SE)\u003c/A\u003e to keep receiving the latest security updates.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cEM\u003eIf you have already purchased the Period 2 ESU\u003c/EM\u003e and need information on accessing the latest Security Updates, please contact us by sending an email to \u003cA href=\"mailto:ExchangeandSfBServerESUInquiry@service.microsoft.com?subject=We%20purchased%20Exchange%20ESU%20need%20access\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eExchangeandSfBServerESUInquiry@service.microsoft.com\u003c/A\u003e.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cH3\u003eKnown issues with this release\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/update/2026/5126672\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003ePublished calendar (.ics) returns HTTP 500 for calendar applications | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e – to be resolved in a future update\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/update/2026/5130098\" target=\"_blank\"\u003eContentEngine deadlock because of missing Korean WordBreaker rule files | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e – an issue impacting customers with emails in Korean language, to be addressed in a future update\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cH3\u003eIssues resolved in this release\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/hotfix/2026/5105719\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eWrapper messages appear in shared mailbox inbox in hybrid environments | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003e\u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/exchange/server/update/2026/5127092\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eAvailability (free/busy) fails for delegated mailboxes in Exchange hybrid deployments using Graph API only | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cH3\u003eUpdate installation\u003c/H3\u003e\n\u003cP\u003eThe following update paths are available:\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cLI-IMAGE id=\"Dg0oxJ\" caption=\"false\" alt=\"\" align=\"center\" resized=\"false\" size=\"default\" layout=\"stretch\" name=\"\" src=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/t5/s/gxcuf89792/images/bS00NTYxNzE4LURnMG94Sg?image-dimensions=750x750\u0026amp;revision=4\"\u003e\u003c/LI-IMAGE\u003e\n\u003cUL\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eInventory your Exchange Servers to determine which updates are needed using the \u003cA href=\"https://aka.ms/ExchangeHealthChecker\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eExchange Server Health Checker script\u003c/A\u003e. Running this script will tell you if any of your Exchange Servers are behind on updates (CUs, SUs, or manual actions).\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eInstall the latest CU. Use the\u0026nbsp;\u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://aka.ms/ExchangeUpdateWizard\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eExchange Update Wizard\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e to choose your current CU and your target CU to get directions.\u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eAfter setup is completed, please reboot the server and check that all Exchange services have started properly. If some services are in a disabled state, that indicates that something interrupted installation of the update. Please see the Workaround 1 in \u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/troubleshoot/exchange/client-connectivity/exchange-security-update-issues#services-dont-start-after-su-installation\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003ethis article\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e.\u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003cLI\u003eIf you encounter errors during or after installation of Exchange Server, run the \u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://aka.ms/ExSetupAssist\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eSetupAssist script\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e. If something does not work properly after updates, see \u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://aka.ms/ExchangeFAQ\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eRepair failed installations of Exchange Cumulative and Security updates\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e. Also please see \u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003cA style=\"font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; background-color: rgb(255, 255, 255);\" href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/topic/file-version-error-when-you-try-to-install-exchange-server-november-2024-su-a650da30-f8fb-469d-a449-47396cab0a15\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eFile version error when you try to install Exchange Server updates\u003c/A\u003e\u003cSPAN style=\"color: rgb(30, 30, 30);\"\u003e.\u003c/SPAN\u003e\u003c/LI\u003e\n\u003c/UL\u003e\n\u003cH1\u003eFAQs\u003c/H1\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eOur organization is in Hybrid mode with Exchange Online. Do we need to do anything?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eExchange Online is already protected, but this SU needs to be installed on your Exchange servers, even if they are used only for management purposes. If you change the auth certificate after installing an SU, you should re-run the Hybrid Configuration Wizard.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eThe last SU/HU we installed is a few months old. Do we need to install all SUs in order to install the latest one?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eSUs are cumulative. If you are running a CU supported by the SU, you do not need to install all SUs or HUs in sequential order; simply install the latest SU. Please see\u0026nbsp;\u003cA href=\"https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/t5/exchange-team-blog/why-exchange-server-updates-matter/ba-p/2280770\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003ethis blog post\u003c/A\u003e\u0026nbsp;for more information.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eDo we need to install SUs on all Exchange Servers within our organization? What about ‘Management Tools only’\u0026nbsp;machines?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eOur recommendation is to install SUs on\u0026nbsp;\u003cU\u003eall\u003c/U\u003e\u0026nbsp;Exchange Servers and all servers and workstations running the Exchange Management Tools to ensure compatibility between management tools clients and servers. If you are trying to update the Exchange Management Tools in the environment with no running Exchange servers, please see\u0026nbsp;\u003cA href=\"https://learn.microsoft.com/exchange/manage-hybrid-exchange-recipients-with-management-tools#update-the-exchange-server-management-tools-only-role-with-no-running-exchange-server-to-a-newer-cumulative-or-security-update\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003ethis\u003c/A\u003e.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eWe installed an Exchange SU or HU update and are running Windows Server 2025. How do we uninstall updates as they are not listed on Windows Server 2025?\u003c/STRONG\u003e\u003cBR /\u003eWe do not recommend uninstalling Security Updates. But see \u003cA href=\"https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/servicing/office/can-t-view-or-uninstall-exchange-security-or-hotfix-updates-in-control-panel-on-windows-server-2025\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\"\u003eCan't view or uninstall Exchange security or hotfix updates in Control Panel on Windows Server 2025 | Microsoft Support\u003c/A\u003e.\u003c/P\u003e\n\u003cP\u003e\u003cSTRONG\u003eOur organization does not have the Exchange 2016 and 2019 Period 2 ESU. word-break: break-word;\r\n          padding: 8px 0;\r\n          margin: 0;\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n.c-uhff-base {\r\n  background: var(--lia-bs-gray-100, #f2f2f2);\r\n  margin: 0 auto;\r\n  max-width: calc(1600px + 10%);\r\n  padding: 30px 5% 16px;\r\n  \u0026:before,\r\n  \u0026:after {\r\n    content: ' ';\r\n    display: table;\r\n  }\r\n  \u0026:after {\r\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n   Sidebar share rail\r\n   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n   Uses structural selectors (`.social-share \u003e button`, `\u003e div`, `\u003e a`) so\r\n   inner styles land regardless of CSS Modules class hashing. Panel reveal\r\n   uses `!important` to override Bootstrap's global `[hidden]` rule. */\r\n\r\n.social-share {\r\n  position: fixed;\r\n  top: 50%;\r\n  left: 4px;\r\n  transform: translateY(-50%);\r\n  z-index: 1000;\r\n  width: 280px;\r\n  height: 36px;\r\n  pointer-events: none;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Toggle button (first direct \u003cbutton\u003e child) */\r\n.social-share \u003e button {\r\n  position: absolute;\r\n  top: 0;\r\n  left: 0;\r\n  z-index: 1;\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  justify-content: center;\r\n  width: 36px;\r\n  height: 36px;\r\n  border: none;\r\n  border-radius: 10px;\r\n  background: var(--lia-bs-body-bg, #ffffff);\r\n  box-shadow:\r\n    0 2px 6px rgba(var(--lia-bs-black-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.08),\r\n    0 8px 20px rgba(var(--lia-bs-black-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.08);\r\n  cursor: pointer;\r\n  color: var(--lia-bs-body-color, #242424);\r\n  pointer-events: auto;\r\n  transition:\r\n    opacity 0.15s ease,\r\n    background-color 0.2s ease,\r\n    box-shadow 0.2s ease;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share \u003e button:hover {\r\n  background: var(--lia-bs-gray-100, #f5f5f5);\r\n  box-shadow:\r\n    0 4px 10px rgba(var(--lia-bs-black-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.1),\r\n    0 12px 28px rgba(var(--lia-bs-black-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.1);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share \u003e button:focus-visible {\r\n  /* #5b5fc7 is a brand social color (per exception list) — not tokenized */\r\n  outline: 2px solid #5b5fc7;\r\n  outline-offset: 2px;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Panel (direct \u003cdiv\u003e child of container — only div child in the DOM) */\r\n.social-share \u003e div {\r\n  position: absolute;\r\n  /* Vertically centre the panel around the button's position so it doesn't\r\n     get cut off at the bottom of the viewport when the rail is near mid-height. */\r\n  top: 50%;\r\n  left: 0;\r\n  transform: translateY(-50%);\r\n  z-index: 2;\r\n  display: none;\r\n  flex-direction: column;\r\n  background: var(--lia-bs-body-bg, #ffffff);\r\n  border-radius: 16px;\r\n  box-shadow:\r\n    0 4px 10px rgba(var(--lia-bs-black-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.06),\r\n    0 16px 40px rgba(var(--lia-bs-black-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.12);\r\n  padding: 8px;\r\n  min-width: 220px;\r\n  gap: 0;\r\n  pointer-events: auto;\r\n  /* Cap height to the viewport and scroll if still too tall (small screens). */\r\n  max-height: calc(100vh - 32px);\r\n  overflow-y: auto;\r\n  overscroll-behavior: contain;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Reveal panel on hover / focus / click-toggle — !important to override\r\n   Bootstrap's global [hidden] { display: none !important; }.\r\n   `[data-expanded=\"true\"]` is set by the inline script when the user clicks\r\n   or taps the button (mobile / non-hover interactions). */\r\n.social-share:hover \u003e div,\r\n.social-share:focus-within \u003e div,\r\n.social-share[data-expanded='true'] \u003e div {\r\n  display: flex !important;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share:hover \u003e button,\r\n.social-share:focus-within \u003e button,\r\n.social-share[data-expanded='true'] \u003e button {\r\n  opacity: 0;\r\n  pointer-events: none;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Esc-close override: after Esc, the JS adds `data-esc-closed=\"true\"` so\r\n   even a hovering mouse cannot re-open the panel. Cleared on mouseleave. */\r\n.social-share[data-esc-closed='true'] \u003e div {\r\n  display: none !important;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share[data-esc-closed='true'] \u003e button {\r\n  opacity: 1;\r\n  pointer-events: auto;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Panel internals — heading (first \u003cspan\u003e child of panel) */\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e span {\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  gap: 14px;\r\n  padding: 10px 12px 12px;\r\n  font-size: 15px;\r\n  font-weight: 600;\r\n  color: var(--lia-bs-body-color, #242424);\r\n  line-height: 1.2;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Every share row (each \u003ca\u003e child of panel) */\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e a {\r\n  display: flex;\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  gap: 14px;\r\n  padding: 10px 12px;\r\n  border-radius: 8px;\r\n  border: 2px solid transparent;\r\n  text-decoration: none;\r\n  color: var(--lia-bs-body-color, #242424);\r\n  font-size: 14px;\r\n  line-height: 1.2;\r\n  transition:\r\n    background-color 0.15s ease,\r\n    border-color 0.15s ease;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e a:hover {\r\n  /* #f0f0fb / #5b5fc7 are brand social colors (per exception list) — not tokenized */\r\n  background: #f0f0fb;\r\n  border-color: #5b5fc7;\r\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #5b5fc7 inset;\r\n  text-decoration: none;\r\n  color: var(--lia-bs-body-color, #242424);\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e a:focus {\r\n  outline: none;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e a:focus-visible {\r\n  /* brand social colors (per exception list) — not tokenized */\r\n  outline: none;\r\n  border-color: #5b5fc7;\r\n  background: #f0f0fb;\r\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #5b5fc7 inset;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Icon slot (first \u003cspan\u003e inside heading or link) */\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e span \u003e span:first-child,\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e a \u003e span:first-child {\r\n  display: inline-flex;\r\n  align-items: center;\r\n  justify-content: center;\r\n  width: 28px;\r\n  height: 28px;\r\n  flex-shrink: 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e span \u003e span:first-child svg,\r\n.social-share \u003e div \u003e a \u003e span:first-child svg {\r\n  display: block;\r\n {\r\n  white-space: nowrap;\r\n}\r\n\r\n@media (max-width: 991px) {\r\n  .social-share {\r\n    display: none;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n","texts":{"heading.whatsNew":"What's new","heading.store":"Microsoft
